## Verification Complete

**Status:** gaps_found
**Score:** 7/8 must-haves verified
**Report:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/.planning/phases/36-inline-upload-progress/36-VERIFICATION.md`

### Gaps Found

1 gap blocking full goal achievement:

**"Clicking retry resets visual state AND re-triggers the actual upload via useDropUpload (D-09)"** — PARTIAL

The retry data flow is correctly wired (`handleRetry` -> `retryFile` + `onRetry` -> `FileBrowser.handleRetryUpload` -> `handleFileDrop`), but `UploadListItem.tsx` has a React Rules of Hooks violation:

- The three `useCallback` hooks (`handleCancel`, `handleDismiss`, `handleRetry`) at lines 54-70 are defined **after** the `if (!file) return null` early return at line 47
- When `removeFile(fileId)` is called during the 1-second completion flash timer, `files.get(fileId)` returns `undefined`, the component re-renders, hits the early return, and skips those hook calls
- React throws "Rendered more hooks than previous render" in development mode

**Fix:** Move the three `useCallback` declarations to before line 47.

**All other verifications passed:**
- TypeScript: no errors (`tsc -b` clean)
- Tests: 23/23 passing including all 12 upload error recovery tests
- Old popup components (`UploadModal.tsx`, `UploadItem.tsx`) confirmed deleted
- No popup CSS classes remaining in codebase
- All per-file store actions wired correctly throughout the upload pipeline
- `targetFolderId` filtering in `FileList.tsx` confirmed (upload rows scoped to correct folder)
- Completion flash timer with `useRef` cleanup present
- No `rgba()` legacy CSS syntax

Structured gap in VERIFICATION.md frontmatter for `/gsd:plan-phase --gaps`.

When a file is removed from the store (e.g., during the completion flash timer), the component re-renders, hits the early return, skips those hooks, and React throws "Rendered more hooks than previous render."

1	import { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
2	import { useUploadStore } from '../../stores/upload.store';
3	
4	type UploadListItemProps = {
5	  /** Upload entry ID in the upload store */
6	  fileId: string;
7	  /** Callback to re-trigger the upload for a failed file (passes the original File object) */
8	  onRetry?: (file: File) => void;
9	};
10	
11	/**
12	 * Inline upload progress row that matches the FileListItem grid layout.
13	 *
14	 * Subscribes to a single file entry via fine-grained Zustand selector
15	 * so progress updates only re-render this row, not the entire FileList.
16	 *
17	 * States:
18	 * - encrypting: pulsing indeterminate progress bar, cancel button
19	 * - uploading: determinate progress bar 0-100%, cancel button
20	 * - complete: green flash for 1s, then removed from store
21	 * - error: red progress bar, retry + dismiss buttons
22	 */
23	export function UploadListItem({ fileId, onRetry }: UploadListItemProps) {
24	  const file = useUploadStore((s) => s.files.get(fileId));
25	  const cancelFile = useUploadStore((s) => s.cancelFile);
26	  const removeFile = useUploadStore((s) => s.removeFile);
27	  const retryFile = useUploadStore((s) => s.retryFile);
28	
29	  // Timer ref for completion flash (D-05, D-06)
30	  const completionTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();
31	
32	  // When status becomes 'complete', start 1000ms timer then remove (D-05)
33	  useEffect(() => {
34	    if (file?.status === 'complete') {
35	      completionTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
36	        removeFile(fileId);
37	      }, 1000);
38	    }
39	
40	    return () => {
41	      if (completionTimerRef.current) {
42	        clearTimeout(completionTimerRef.current);
43	      }
44	    };
45	  }, [file?.status, fileId, removeFile]);
46	
47	  if (!file) return null;
48	
49	  const isError = file.status === 'error';
50	  const isComplete = file.status === 'complete';
51	  const isEncrypting = file.status === 'encrypting';
52	
53	  // Cancel handler: cancel the network request and remove from UI immediately (D-07, D-08)
54	  const handleCancel = useCallback(() => {
55	    cancelFile(fileId);
56	    removeFile(fileId);
57	  }, [fileId, cancelFile, removeFile]);
58	
59	  // Dismiss handler: remove failed upload row from UI (D-10)
60	  const handleDismiss = useCallback(() => {
61	    removeFile(fileId);
62	  }, [fileId, removeFile]);
63	
64	  // Retry handler: reset visual state AND re-trigger the actual upload (D-09)
65	  const handleRetry = useCallback(() => {
66	    retryFile(fileId);
67	    if (file?.file && onRetry) {
68	      onRetry(file.file);
69	    }
70	  }, [fileId, retryFile, file?.file, onRetry]);
71	
72	  const rowClassName = [
73	    'file-list-item',
74	    'upload-inline-row',
75	    isError ? 'upload-inline-row--error' : '',
76	    isComplete ? 'upload-inline-row--complete' : '',
77	  ]
78	    .filter(Boolean)
79	    .join(' ');
80	
81	  return (
82	    <div className={rowClassName} role="row">
83	      {/* Row top: icon + name + progress bar */}
84	      <div className="file-list-item-row-top" role="gridcell">
85	        <span className="file-list-item-icon upload-inline-icon" aria-hidden="true">
86	          {isError ? '[!]' : '[^]'}
87	        </span>
88	        <div className="upload-inline-name-wrapper">
89	          <span className="file-list-item-name">{file.filename}</span>
90	          <div
91	            className={`upload-inline-progress-track ${isEncrypting ? 'upload-inline-progress-track--pulse' : ''}`}
92	          >
93	            <div
94	              className="upload-inline-progress-fill"
95	              style={{ width: `${file.progress}%` }}
96	              data-status={file.status}
97	              role="progressbar"
98	              aria-valuenow={file.progress}
99	              aria-valuemin={0}
100	              aria-valuemax={100}
101	              aria-label={`Upload progress for ${file.filename}`}
102	            />
103	          </div>
104	        </div>
105	      </div>
106	
107	      {/* Row bottom: size + date/actions */}
108	      <div className="file-list-item-row-bottom">
109	        <span className="file-list-item-size" role="gridcell">
110	          {'--'}
111	        </span>
112	        <span className="file-list-item-date upload-inline-actions" role="gridcell">
113	          {/* Cancel button -- visible during encrypting/uploading */}
114	          {!isComplete && !isError && (
115	            <button
116	              type="button"
117	              className="upload-inline-btn"
118	              onClick={handleCancel}
119	              aria-label={`Cancel upload of ${file.filename}`}
120	              title="Cancel upload"
121	            >
122	              {'[x]'}
123	            </button>
124	          )}
125	          {/* Error state -- retry + dismiss */}
126	          {isError && (
127	            <>
128	              <button
129	                type="button"
130	                className="upload-inline-btn upload-inline-btn--retry"
131	                onClick={handleRetry}
132	                aria-label={`Retry upload of ${file.filename}`}
133	                title="Retry upload"
134	              >
135	                {'[R]'}
136	              </button>
137	              <button
138	                type="button"
139	                className="upload-inline-btn"
140	                onClick={handleDismiss}
141	                aria-label={`Dismiss failed upload of ${file.filename}`}
142	                title="Dismiss error"
143	              >
144	                {'[x]'}
145	              </button>
146	            </>
147	          )}
148	        </span>
149	      </div>
150	    </div>
151	  );
152	}
153	
```

Now I have a comprehensive view of all the files. Let me compile my findings.

---

Here are the code quality findings for PR 410:

## 1. Redundant State: `setFileComplete` is a duplicate of `setFileStatus`

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/stores/upload.store.ts:91-98`

`setFileComplete(id)` does exactly the same thing as `setFileStatus(id, 'complete')` -- the `setFileStatus` implementation already handles `status === 'complete'` by setting `progress: 100` (lines 84-85). Both methods clone the Map, look up the entry, spread-merge, and set. Having both creates a maintenance hazard where behavior could diverge.

**Fix:** Remove `setFileComplete` from the store interface and implementation. Replace all call sites with `setFileStatus(id, 'complete')`. There are 3 call sites: `useDropUpload.ts:134`, `useDropUpload.ts:176`, and `upload.service.ts:122`.

## 2. Copy-Paste: Upload ID generation + addFile + cancel-check pattern repeated 3 times

**Files:**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useDropUpload.ts:114-119` (new files loop)
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useDropUpload.ts:150-155` (duplicate files loop)
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/upload.service.ts:106-111`

The exact same 4-line block is repeated three times:
```ts
const uploadId = `upload-${file.name}-${Date.now()}`;
currentUploadId = uploadId;                              // (only in useDropUpload)
useUploadStore.getState().addFile(uploadId, file.name, folderId, file);
if (useUploadStore.getState().files.get(uploadId)?.status === 'cancelled') {
  throw new Error('Upload cancelled by user');
}
```

**Fix:** Extract a helper function, e.g. `registerUpload(file, folderId)` that generates the ID, adds the file to the store, checks for immediate cancellation, and returns the `uploadId`. This eliminates the ID format string (`upload-${file.name}-${Date.now()}`) being hardcoded in three places -- if the format ever changes, only one location needs updating.

## 3. Redundant State: `isUploading` derived identically in two hooks

**Files:**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useDropUpload.ts:30-34`
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useFileUpload.ts:22-24`

Both hooks derive `isUploading` / `hasActiveUpload` by iterating the same Map with the same predicate `f.status === 'encrypting' || f.status === 'uploading'`. The literal string pair `'encrypting' || 'uploading'` appears 5 times total across the codebase (also in `useFileUpload.ts:67` for the cancel action, and `upload.store.ts:136` in reset).

**Fix:** Add a derived selector or utility, e.g. `const isActive = (s: PerFileUpload) => s.status === 'encrypting' || s.status === 'uploading'` exported from `upload.store.ts`. Alternatively, add a computed `hasActiveUploads` selector on the store itself.

## 4. Dead Code: `useFileUpload` hook is exported but has zero consumers

**Files:**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useFileUpload.ts` (entire file)
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/index.ts:10`

`useFileUpload` is only referenced from its own file and the barrel export. No component or hook imports it. The entire hook, along with its backward-compatibility derived state (`status`, `progress`, `currentFile`, `totalFiles`, `completedFiles`, `error`), is unused code. The `upload.service.ts::uploadFiles()` function it wraps is also not called by anything else after this PR removed the old UploadModal flow.

**Fix:** If this hook has no consumers and isn't intended as a public API for future use, remove it (and possibly `upload.service.ts::uploadFiles` too). If it's intentionally preserved for future use, add a comment documenting why.

## 5. Missing Zustand Equality Function: Selector in FileList creates a new array every render cycle

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/FileList.tsx:115-123`

The Zustand selector creates a new `entries` array on every store change (not just upload-related changes). Since Zustand uses `Object.is` by default, this selector will return a new reference every time, causing `FileList` to re-render on **every** store update -- even those unrelated to the current folder's uploads. This defeats the stated design goal in the comment on line 113-114 about fine-grained subscriptions.

```ts
const uploadEntries = useUploadStore((s) => {
  const entries: { ... }[] = [];
  for (const f of s.files.values()) { ... }
  return entries;  // Always a new array reference
});
```

**Fix:** Pass `shallow` from `zustand/shallow` as the equality function: `useUploadStore((s) => { ... }, shallow)`. This ensures re-renders only happen when the array contents actually change.

## 6. Unstable `Date.now()` in virtual entries causes unnecessary re-sorts

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/FileList.tsx:126-134`

`uploadVirtualEntries` sets `createdAt: Date.now()` and `modifiedAt: Date.now()`. These values are recalculated every render, producing new timestamps each time. While the current `sortItems` function only sorts by type and name (not by date), this creates unstable object identity and could cause subtle bugs if sorting ever incorporates dates. More importantly, these fields exist solely to satisfy the `FolderChild` type shape for the `as FolderChild[]` cast on line 146 -- they carry no semantic meaning.

**Fix:** Use a stable sentinel value like `0` instead of `Date.now()`.

## 7. Unnecessary Comments: Several comments explain WHAT not WHY

**Files (selected examples):**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/UploadListItem.tsx:81`: `{/* Row top: icon + name + progress bar */}` -- this is visible from the JSX structure
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/UploadListItem.tsx:105`: `{/* Row bottom: size + date/actions */}` -- same
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/UploadListItem.tsx:111`: `{/* Cancel button -- visible during encrypting/uploading */}` -- the `!isComplete && !isError` condition makes this self-evident
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/FileList.tsx:148`: `{/* Select-all uses real item count only (upload rows are not selectable) */}` -- This one is borderline; the "not selectable" part explains WHY, but could be more concise

These are low severity. The comments in `UploadListItem.tsx` at lines 29 and 32 (referencing D-05, D-06, D-09 design spec IDs) are good -- they explain WHY with traceability.

## 8. Leaky Abstraction: `handleCancel` calls both `cancelFile` and `removeFile` -- should be one store action

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/UploadListItem.tsx:48-51`

```ts
const handleCancel = useCallback(() => {
  cancelFile(fileId);
  removeFile(fileId);
}, [fileId, cancelFile, removeFile]);
```

The component is forced to know that cancelling requires two store operations in sequence. This is a race: between `cancelFile` and `removeFile`, a progress callback from the upload could theoretically update the file entry (since `cancelFile` sets status to `'cancelled'` but doesn't remove it). `cancelFile` in the store should either also remove the entry, or there should be a single `cancelAndRemoveFile` action.

**Fix:** Either have `cancelFile` in the store also delete the entry from the map after cancelling, or add a `cancelAndRemoveFile(id)` action that does both atomically in a single `set()` call.

---

**Summary:** 2 high-impact issues (finding 5: missing shallow equality causing excessive re-renders; finding 4: dead code), 3 medium issues (findings 1, 2, 3: redundancy and copy-paste), and 3 low-severity issues (findings 6, 7, 8).

Now I have a comprehensive picture. Let me analyze all the efficiency issues.

---

Here are the efficiency findings:

## EFFICIENCY ISSUES

### 1. Re-render storm: FileList's Zustand selector creates new array reference on every store update (HIGH)

**File:** `apps/web/src/components/file-browser/FileList.tsx:115-123`

**Issue:** The `useUploadStore` selector inside `FileList` creates a new array on every call. Zustand uses `Object.is` for equality by default, so a new array `[]` !== previous `[]`. Every `updateFileProgress` call (which fires many times per second during upload) triggers the selector, which returns a new array reference, which re-renders `FileList` -- even if no entries were added/removed and no status changed. This re-renders all `FileListItem` children too, not just the upload rows.

The comment says "Progress updates re-render individual UploadListItem rows via their own fine-grained selectors" but this is defeated because the `FileList` selector re-runs on every store change and always returns a new array, causing the entire list to re-render.

```typescript
const uploadEntries = useUploadStore((s) => {
  const entries: { id: string; filename: string; status: PerFileUpload['status'] }[] = [];
  for (const f of s.files.values()) {
    if (f.targetFolderId === parentId && f.status !== 'cancelled') {
      entries.push({ id: f.id, filename: f.filename, status: f.status });
    }
  }
  return entries; // New array every time -- always triggers re-render
});
```

**Fix:** Use `zustand/shallow` as the equality function, or implement a custom equality check:

```typescript
import { shallow } from 'zustand/shallow';

const uploadEntries = useUploadStore(
  (s) => {
    const entries: { id: string; filename: string; status: PerFileUpload['status'] }[] = [];
    for (const f of s.files.values()) {
      if (f.targetFolderId === parentId && f.status !== 'cancelled') {
        entries.push({ id: f.id, filename: f.filename, status: f.status });
      }
    }
    return entries;
  },
  shallow
);
```

`shallow` does a shallow comparison of array elements, so it will return the same reference when the entries are structurally identical. However, since each element is an object `{ id, filename, status }`, `shallow` only compares top-level elements by reference. A better approach is a custom comparator that compares the serialized key data, or memoize differently. Actually, `shallow` on arrays compares each element with `Object.is`, which still fails since each element is a new object. The most robust fix:

```typescript
const uploadEntries = useUploadStore((s) => {
  const entries: { id: string; filename: string; status: PerFileUpload['status'] }[] = [];
  for (const f of s.files.values()) {
    if (f.targetFolderId === parentId && f.status !== 'cancelled') {
      entries.push({ id: f.id, filename: f.filename, status: f.status });
    }
  }
  return entries;
}, (a, b) => {
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
    if (a[i].id !== b[i].id || a[i].status !== b[i].status) return false;
  }
  return true;
});
```

This ensures FileList only re-renders when entries are added/removed or their status changes -- not on every progress tick.

---

### 2. No-op store updates: `updateFileProgress` fires `set()` on every progress tick without change guard (MEDIUM)

**File:** `apps/web/src/stores/upload.store.ts:69-76`

**Issue:** `updateFileProgress` is called from the upload progress callback, potentially many times per second. It always clones the Map and creates a new entry object even if `progress` hasn't changed (e.g., two consecutive calls with `progress=42`). Each `set()` notifies all subscribers.

```typescript
updateFileProgress: (id, progress) =>
  set((state) => {
    const entry = state.files.get(id);
    if (!entry) return state;
    const next = new Map(state.files);  // Clone entire Map every tick
    next.set(id, { ...entry, status: 'uploading', progress });
    return { files: next };
  }),
```

**Fix:** Add a same-value guard:

```typescript
updateFileProgress: (id, progress) =>
  set((state) => {
    const entry = state.files.get(id);
    if (!entry) return state;
    if (entry.status === 'uploading' && entry.progress === progress) return state;
    const next = new Map(state.files);
    next.set(id, { ...entry, status: 'uploading', progress });
    return { files: next };
  }),
```

This avoids cloning the Map and notifying subscribers when the progress value hasn't actually changed.

---

### 3. Redundant Map cloning: Every per-file action clones the entire Map (LOW-MEDIUM)

**File:** `apps/web/src/stores/upload.store.ts:53-130` (all actions: `addFile`, `updateFileProgress`, `setFileStatus`, `setFileComplete`, `removeFile`, `cancelFile`, `retryFile`)

**Issue:** Every action does `new Map(state.files)` which copies all entries. With N files in progress, each progress tick copies N entries even though only one changed. This is O(N) per update. For a typical batch of 5-10 files this is negligible, but combined with high-frequency progress callbacks it adds up.

**Fix:** This is acceptable for the expected scale (single-digit concurrent uploads). Noting for completeness. If performance becomes an issue, consider using Immer or a different data structure. The same-value guard from finding #2 is more impactful since it eliminates unnecessary clones entirely.

---

### 4. `useFileUpload` re-derives batch state on every render without memoization (MEDIUM)

**File:** `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileUpload.ts:14-26`

**Issue:** `useFileUpload` subscribes to the entire `files` Map via `useUploadStore((s) => s.files)`. This means every progress tick (which creates a new Map) re-renders every component using `useFileUpload`. Inside the render, it re-derives all batch-level state:

```typescript
const fileEntries = Array.from(files.values());         // allocates new array
const hasActiveUpload = fileEntries.some(...)            // scans array
const hasError = fileEntries.some(...)                   // scans array  
const activeFile = fileEntries.find(...)                 // scans array
// ... and in the return:
completedFiles: fileEntries.filter((f) => ...).length,   // scans + allocates
error: fileEntries.find((f) => f.error)?.error ?? null,  // scans array
```

This runs on every progress tick because the `files` Map reference changes.

**Fix:** Use `useMemo` for the derived values, or better yet, use a Zustand selector that returns only the derived scalar values with a shallow comparator:

```typescript
const { status, progress, currentFile, totalFiles, completedFiles, error, isUploading } = 
  useUploadStore((s) => {
    const entries = Array.from(s.files.values());
    const hasActive = entries.some(f => f.status === 'encrypting' || f.status === 'uploading');
    const hasErr = entries.some(f => f.status === 'error');
    const active = entries.find(f => f.status === 'encrypting' || f.status === 'uploading');
    return {
      status: hasErr ? 'error' : hasActive ? 'uploading' : 'idle',
      progress: active?.progress ?? 0,
      currentFile: active?.filename ?? null,
      totalFiles: entries.length,
      completedFiles: entries.filter(f => f.status === 'complete').length,
      error: entries.find(f => f.error)?.error ?? null,
      isUploading: hasActive,
    };
  }, shallow);
```

This still runs the derivation logic on every store change, but `shallow` prevents re-renders when the derived values haven't changed (e.g., progress went from 42 to 43 but this hook's consumer only cares about `isUploading`).

---

### 5. `useDropUpload` subscribes to entire `files` Map just to derive `isUploading` boolean (MEDIUM)

**File:** `apps/web/src/hooks/useDropUpload.ts:29-34`

**Issue:** The hook subscribes to the entire `files` Map to derive a single boolean. Every progress tick creates a new Map, which re-triggers this selector, which re-renders the component.

```typescript
const uploadFiles = useUploadStore((s) => s.files);
const isUploading =
  uploadFiles.size > 0 &&
  Array.from(uploadFiles.values()).some(
    (f) => f.status === 'encrypting' || f.status === 'uploading'
  );
```

**Fix:** Move the derivation into the selector and return a boolean (primitives use `Object.is` comparison, so no extra equality function needed):

```typescript
const isUploading = useUploadStore((s) => {
  for (const f of s.files.values()) {
    if (f.status === 'encrypting' || f.status === 'uploading') return true;
  }
  return false;
});
```

This prevents re-renders on progress ticks since the boolean value doesn't change while an upload is active.

---

### 6. `Date.now()` called per virtual entry on every render (LOW)

**File:** `apps/web/src/components/file-browser/FileList.tsx:126-134`

**Issue:** Each `uploadVirtualEntries` mapping calls `Date.now()` twice per entry. Since these virtual entries are re-created on every render (due to issue #1), `createdAt` and `modifiedAt` change every time. If `sortItems` ever sorts by date, this would cause unstable sort order. Currently sort is by name so it's not a functional bug, just unnecessary computation.

```typescript
const uploadVirtualEntries: UploadVirtualEntry[] = uploadEntries.map((f) => ({
  ...
  createdAt: Date.now(),   // different value every render
  modifiedAt: Date.now(),  // different value every render
  _uploading: true as const,
}));
```

**Fix:** Use a stable timestamp (e.g., `0` or a memoized value):

```typescript
createdAt: 0,
modifiedAt: 0,
```

---

### 7. `File` object retained in store indefinitely for errored uploads (LOW-MEDIUM)

**File:** `apps/web/src/stores/upload.store.ts:25` and `apps/web/src/components/file-browser/UploadListItem.tsx:33-45`

**Issue:** The `PerFileUpload.file` field holds a reference to the original `File` object (for retry). For completed uploads, the `UploadListItem` auto-removes after 1s. But for errored or cancelled uploads that the user never dismisses, the `File` object (potentially up to 100MB) stays in memory indefinitely. The `cancelFile` action in `UploadListItem` calls both `cancelFile` and `removeFile` so cancelled files are cleaned up, but errored files persist until the user clicks dismiss or retry.

**Fix:** This is acceptable since errored files need the `File` reference for retry. However, consider adding a TTL-based cleanup (e.g., remove errored entries after 5 minutes) or clearing the `file` reference after a timeout while keeping the error display.

---

### 8. `setFileComplete` is redundant with `setFileStatus(id, 'complete')` (LOW)

**File:** `apps/web/src/stores/upload.store.ts:91-98` vs `78-89`

**Issue:** `setFileComplete` does `{ ...entry, status: 'complete', progress: 100 }` while `setFileStatus` already handles `status === 'complete'` by setting `progress: 100`. They're functionally identical but having both means maintaining two code paths.

**Fix:** Remove `setFileComplete` and use `setFileStatus(id, 'complete')` everywhere, or keep it as a convenience wrapper that delegates to `setFileStatus`.

---

### Summary of high-impact fixes:

1. **#1 (HIGH):** FileList selector needs a custom equality comparator to prevent re-rendering the entire file list on every progress tick
2. **#2 (MEDIUM):** `updateFileProgress` needs a same-value guard to avoid no-op store updates
3. **#5 (MEDIUM):** `useDropUpload` should derive `isUploading` inside the selector as a boolean, not subscribe to the entire Map
4. **#4 (MEDIUM):** `useFileUpload` should use a shallow-compared selector returning derived scalars

Issues #1 and #2 together create a cascade: every progress callback clones the Map (#2), which triggers the FileList selector (#1), which returns a new array, which re-renders the entire file list including all `FileListItem` children. Fixing both eliminates the hot-path performance issue.

Now I have a thorough understanding of the codebase. Let me compile my findings.

---

Here is my structured review of code reuse issues in PR 410:

## Code Reuse Findings

### 1. Duplicated `sortItems` function (pre-existing, but worth noting)

**File:** `apps/web/src/components/file-browser/FileList.tsx` (lines 60-69)
**Also in:** `apps/web/src/components/file-browser/SharedFileBrowser.tsx` (lines 40-46)

The `sortItems` function is identically defined in both files (folders first, then alphabetical by name). This is a pre-existing duplication, not introduced by this PR. However, the PR now passes `UploadVirtualEntry[]` through this same function via a cast (`as FolderChild[]`), which further couples it. If `sortItems` were extracted to a shared utility (e.g., `utils/sort.ts`), it could be typed more broadly.

**Verdict:** Pre-existing. Not introduced by this PR. Low priority.

### 2. Duplicated upload ID generation pattern

**Files:**
- `apps/web/src/hooks/useDropUpload.ts` -- lines 114, 150 (`` `upload-${file.name}-${Date.now()}` ``)
- `apps/web/src/services/upload.service.ts` -- line 106 (`` `upload-${file.name}-${Date.now()}` ``)

The upload ID format `` `upload-${file.name}-${Date.now()}` `` is generated in 3 separate locations. The `PerFileUpload` type even documents the format in a comment (`id: string; // format: \`upload-${filename}-${Date.now()}\``). This should be a small utility function in the upload store or a shared location:

```ts
function createUploadId(filename: string): string {
  return `upload-${filename}-${Date.now()}`;
}
```

**Suggested fix:** Extract a `createUploadId(filename: string)` helper, either exported from `upload.store.ts` or as part of a shared upload utility module. This ensures the format stays consistent if it ever needs to change (e.g., to add a random suffix for collision avoidance when two files with the same name are uploaded within the same millisecond).

### 3. Repeated "is active upload" status check

**Files:**
- `apps/web/src/hooks/useDropUpload.ts` line 33: `(f) => f.status === 'encrypting' || f.status === 'uploading'`
- `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileUpload.ts` line 23: `(f) => f.status === 'encrypting' || f.status === 'uploading'`
- `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileUpload.ts` line 26: `(f) => f.status === 'encrypting' || f.status === 'uploading'`
- `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileUpload.ts` line 67: `if (f.status === 'encrypting' || f.status === 'uploading')`
- `apps/web/src/stores/upload.store.ts` line 136: `if (entry.cancelSource && (entry.status === 'encrypting' || entry.status === 'uploading'))`

The same two-status disjunction appears 5 times across 3 files. This is a semantic concept ("is this upload actively in progress?") that should be a small predicate:

```ts
export function isActiveUpload(status: PerFileUpload['status']): boolean {
  return status === 'encrypting' || status === 'uploading';
}
```

**Suggested fix:** Add `isActiveUpload` as a helper exported from `upload.store.ts` and use it in all 5 locations.

### 4. No issues with icon usage -- UploadListItem correctly avoids `getItemIcon`

The new `UploadListItem` uses hardcoded `[!]` and `[^]` icons instead of the existing `getItemIcon()` from `utils/format.ts`. This is **correct** -- `getItemIcon` accepts `'file' | 'folder'` and an optional mimeType, and doesn't have upload-specific icons. No reuse issue here.

### 5. No issues with progress bar -- correctly avoids abstracting

The progress bar in `UploadListItem` is implemented with plain `div` elements plus CSS classes. Other progress bars in the codebase (MFA wizard, migration, storage quota, audio/video seek) are all similarly implemented as inline divs with their own CSS classes. There is no shared `ProgressBar` component in the codebase, and creating one just for this would be over-engineering given the varied semantics.

### 6. className builder pattern is fine

The `UploadListItem` uses `[...].filter(Boolean).join(' ')` for className construction (lines 70-77). `FileListItem` uses the same pattern (lines 329-335). Since the project does not use `clsx` or any className utility, this is the established pattern and is acceptable.

## Summary

| # | File | Line(s) | Issue | Severity |
|---|------|---------|-------|----------|
| 1 | `useDropUpload.ts`, `upload.service.ts` | 114, 150, 106 | Upload ID generation (`` `upload-${file.name}-${Date.now()}` ``) duplicated in 3 locations | Medium |
| 2 | `useDropUpload.ts`, `useFileUpload.ts`, `upload.store.ts` | 33, 23, 26, 67, 136 | "Is active upload" check (`status === 'encrypting' \|\| status === 'uploading'`) repeated 5 times | Medium |

Both issues are straightforward to fix: extract a `createUploadId()` function and an `isActiveUpload()` predicate, both from `upload.store.ts`. No other code reuse issues were found -- the PR does not duplicate any existing utilities for formatting, icons, sorting, or UI components.

**High-impact fixes:**
1. FileList selector — new array every render, needs equality comparator
2. `updateFileProgress` — no same-value guard, clones Map on every tick

4. `setFileComplete` — redundant with `setFileStatus(id, 'complete')`

1	import { create } from 'zustand';
2	import axios from 'axios';
3	
4	export type PendingReplacement = {
5	  fileName: string;
6	  fileId: string;
7	  parentId: string;
8	  encryptedData: {
9	    cid: string;
10	    wrappedKey: string;
11	    iv: string;
12	    size: number;
13	    encryptionMode: 'GCM' | 'CTR';
14	  };
15	};
16	
17	export type PerFileUpload = {
18	  id: string; // format: `upload-${filename}-${Date.now()}`
19	  filename: string;
20	  targetFolderId: string; // folder ID where this file is being uploaded
21	  status: 'encrypting' | 'uploading' | 'complete' | 'error' | 'cancelled';
22	  progress: number; // 0-100
23	  error: string | null;
24	  cancelSource: ReturnType<typeof axios.CancelToken.source> | null;
25	  file: File | null; // Original File reference for retry (D-09)
26	};
27	
28	type UploadState = {
29	  files: Map<string, PerFileUpload>;
30	  pendingReplacements: PendingReplacement[];
31	
32	  // Per-file actions
33	  addFile: (id: string, filename: string, targetFolderId: string, file: File) => void;
34	  updateFileProgress: (id: string, progress: number) => void;
35	  setFileStatus: (id: string, status: PerFileUpload['status'], error?: string) => void;
36	  setFileComplete: (id: string) => void;
37	  removeFile: (id: string) => void;
38	  cancelFile: (id: string) => void;
39	  retryFile: (id: string) => void;
40	
41	  // Batch-level actions (kept for backward compat / derived)
42	  reset: () => void;
43	
44	  // PendingReplacements (unchanged)
45	  setPendingReplacements: (replacements: PendingReplacement[]) => void;
46	  clearPendingReplacements: () => void;
47	};
48	
49	export const useUploadStore = create<UploadState>((set, get) => ({
50	  files: new Map<string, PerFileUpload>(),
51	  pendingReplacements: [],
52	
53	  addFile: (id, filename, targetFolderId, file) =>
54	    set((state) => {
55	      const next = new Map(state.files);
56	      next.set(id, {
57	        id,
58	        filename,
59	        targetFolderId,
60	        status: 'encrypting',
61	        progress: 0,
62	        error: null,
63	        cancelSource: axios.CancelToken.source(),
64	        file,
65	      });
66	      return { files: next };
67	    }),
68	
69	  updateFileProgress: (id, progress) =>
70	    set((state) => {
71	      const entry = state.files.get(id);
72	      if (!entry) return state;
73	      const next = new Map(state.files);
74	      next.set(id, { ...entry, status: 'uploading', progress });
75	      return { files: next };
76	    }),
77	
78	  setFileStatus: (id, status, error) =>
79	    set((state) => {
80	      const entry = state.files.get(id);
81	      if (!entry) return state;
82	      const next = new Map(state.files);
83	      const updates: Partial<PerFileUpload> = { status, error: error ?? null };
84	      if (status === 'complete') {
85	        updates.progress = 100;
86	      }
87	      next.set(id, { ...entry, ...updates });
88	      return { files: next };
89	    }),
90	
91	  setFileComplete: (id) =>
92	    set((state) => {
93	      const entry = state.files.get(id);
94	      if (!entry) return state;
95	      const next = new Map(state.files);
96	      next.set(id, { ...entry, status: 'complete', progress: 100 });
97	      return { files: next };
98	    }),
99	
100	  removeFile: (id) =>
101	    set((state) => {
102	      const next = new Map(state.files);
103	      next.delete(id);
104	      return { files: next };
105	    }),
106	
107	  cancelFile: (id) =>
108	    set((state) => {
109	      const entry = state.files.get(id);
110	      if (!entry) return state;
111	      entry.cancelSource?.cancel('Upload cancelled by user');
112	      const next = new Map(state.files);
113	      next.set(id, { ...entry, status: 'cancelled' });
114	      return { files: next };
115	    }),
116	
117	  retryFile: (id) =>
118	    set((state) => {
119	      const entry = state.files.get(id);
120	      if (!entry) return state;
121	      const next = new Map(state.files);
122	      next.set(id, {
123	        ...entry,
124	        status: 'encrypting',
125	        progress: 0,
126	        error: null,
127	        cancelSource: axios.CancelToken.source(),
128	      });
129	      return { files: next };
130	    }),
131	
132	  reset: () => {
133	    // Cancel all active uploads before clearing
134	    const { files } = get();
135	    for (const entry of files.values()) {
136	      if (entry.cancelSource && (entry.status === 'encrypting' || entry.status === 'uploading')) {
137	        entry.cancelSource.cancel('Upload cancelled by user');
138	      }
139	    }
140	    set({
141	      files: new Map<string, PerFileUpload>(),
142	      pendingReplacements: [],
143	    });
144	  },
145	
146	  setPendingReplacements: (replacements) => set({ pendingReplacements: replacements }),
147	  clearPendingReplacements: () => set({ pendingReplacements: [] }),
148	}));
149

50	  onDropOnFolder?: (items: DragItem[], sourceParentId: string, destFolderId: string) => void;
51	  /** Callback when external files are dropped onto a folder */
52	  onExternalFileDrop?: (files: File[], destFolderId: string) => void;
53	  /** Callback to re-trigger upload for a failed file (retry button in UploadListItem) */
54	  onRetryUpload?: (file: File) => void;
55	};

57	/**
58	 * Sort items: folders first, then files, both alphabetically.
59	 */
60	function sortItems(items: FolderChild[]): FolderChild[] {
61	  return [...items].sort((a, b) => {
62	    // Folders come first
63	    if (a.type === 'folder' && b.type !== 'folder') return -1;
64	    if (a.type !== 'folder' && b.type === 'folder') return 1;
65	
66	    // Within same type, sort alphabetically (case-insensitive)
67	    return a.name.localeCompare(b.name, undefined, { sensitivity: 'base' });
68	  });
69	}
70	
71	/**
72	 * File list component displaying files and folders in a columnar layout.
73	 *
74	 * Renders items sorted by type (folders first) then alphabetically.
75	 * Supports selection, navigation, context menu, and drag operations.
76	 *
77	 * @example
78	 * ```tsx
79	 * function FileBrowser() {
80	 *   const { currentFolder } = useFolderNavigation();
81	 *   const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
82	 *
83	 *   return (
84	 *     <FileList
85	 *       items={currentFolder?.children ?? []}
86	 *       selectedId={selectedId}
87	 *       parentId={currentFolder?.id ?? 'root'}
88	 *       onSelect={setSelectedId}
89	 *       onNavigate={navigateTo}
90	 *       onContextMenu={handleContextMenu}
91	 *       onDragStart={handleDragStart}
92	 *     />
93	 *   );
94	 * }
95	 * ```
96	 */
97	export function FileList({
98	  items,
99	  selectedIds,
100	  parentId,
101	  folderKey,
102	  showParentRow,
103	  onNavigateUp,
104	  onSelect,
105	  onSelectAll,
106	  onNavigate,
107	  onContextMenu,
108	  onDragStart,
109	  onDropOnFolder,
110	  onExternalFileDrop,
111	  onRetryUpload,
112	}: FileListProps) {
113	  // Subscribe to upload files for this folder only (IDs + filenames + status, not progress).
114	  // Progress updates re-render individual UploadListItem rows via their own fine-grained selectors.
115	  const uploadEntries = useUploadStore((s) => {
116	    const entries: { id: string; filename: string; status: PerFileUpload['status'] }[] = [];
117	    for (const f of s.files.values()) {
118	      if (f.targetFolderId === parentId && f.status !== 'cancelled') {
119	        entries.push({ id: f.id, filename: f.filename, status: f.status });
120	      }
121	    }
122	    return entries;
123	  });
124	
125	  // Create virtual entries for sorting alongside real items
126	  const uploadVirtualEntries: UploadVirtualEntry[] = uploadEntries.map((f) => ({
127	    type: 'file' as const,
128	    id: f.id,
129	    name: f.filename,
130	    fileMetaIpnsName: '' as const,
131	    createdAt: Date.now(),
132	    modifiedAt: Date.now(),
133	    _uploading: true as const,
134	  }));
135	
136	  // Filter out real files that match completing uploads to prevent duplicates (Pitfall 5)
137	  const completingNames = new Set(
138	    uploadEntries.filter((e) => e.status === 'complete').map((e) => e.filename)
139	  );
140	  const filteredItems = items.filter(
141	    (item) => !(item.type === 'file' && completingNames.has(item.name))
142	  );
143	
144	  // Merge and sort all items together
145	  const allItems = [...filteredItems, ...uploadVirtualEntries];
146	  const sortedItems = sortItems(allItems as FolderChild[]);
147	
148	  // Select-all uses real item count only (upload rows are not selectable)
149	  const allSelected = items.length > 0 && selectedIds.size === items.length;
150	
151	  return (
152	    <div className="file-list" role="grid">
153	      {/* Header row */}
154	      <div className="file-list-header" role="row">
155	        <div className="file-list-header-name" role="columnheader">
156	          <span
157	            className="file-list-header-checkbox"
158	            onClick={onSelectAll}
159	            role="checkbox"
160	            aria-checked={allSelected}
161	            aria-label={allSelected ? 'Deselect all' : 'Select all'}
162	            tabIndex={0}
163	            onKeyDown={(e) => {
164	              if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
165	                e.preventDefault();
166	                onSelectAll();
167	              }
168	            }}
169	          >
170	            {allSelected ? '[x]' : '[ ]'}
171	          </span>
172	          [NAME]
173	        </div>
174	        <div className="file-list-header-size" role="columnheader">
175	          [SIZE]
176	        </div>
177	        <div className="file-list-header-date" role="columnheader">
178	          [MODIFIED]
179	        </div>
180	        {/* Empty header cell for mobile action column - hidden on desktop via CSS */}
181	        <div className="file-list-header-actions" aria-hidden="true" />
182	      </div>
183	
184	      {/* Item rows */}
185	      <div className="file-list-body" role="rowgroup">
186	        {showParentRow && onNavigateUp && <ParentDirRow onActivate={onNavigateUp} />}
187	        {sortedItems.map((item) =>
188	          '_uploading' in item ? (
189	            <UploadListItem key={item.id} fileId={item.id} onRetry={onRetryUpload} />
190	          ) : (
191	            <FileListItem
192	              key={item.id}
193	              item={item}
194	              isSelected={selectedIds.has(item.id)}
195	              parentId={parentId}
196	              selectedIds={selectedIds}
197	              allItems={items}
198	              folderKey={folderKey}
199	              onSelect={onSelect}
200	              onNavigate={onNavigate}
201	              onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
202	              onDragStart={onDragStart}
203	              onDrop={
204	                onDropOnFolder && item.type === 'folder'
205	                  ? (dragItems, sourceParentId) =>
206	                      onDropOnFolder(dragItems, sourceParentId, item.id)
207	                  : undefined
208	              }
209	              onExternalFileDrop={item.type === 'folder' ? onExternalFileDrop : undefined}
210	            />
211	          )
212	        )}
213	      </div>
214	    </div>
215	  );
216	}

2	import { useUploadStore } from '../../stores/upload.store';

4	type UploadListItemProps = {
5	  /** Upload entry ID in the upload store */
6	  fileId: string;
7	  /** Callback to re-trigger the upload for a failed file (passes the original File object) */
8	  onRetry?: (file: File) => void;
9	};
10	
11	/**
12	 * Inline upload progress row that matches the FileListItem grid layout.
13	 *
14	 * Subscribes to a single file entry via fine-grained Zustand selector
15	 * so progress updates only re-render this row, not the entire FileList.
16	 *
17	 * States:
18	 * - encrypting: pulsing indeterminate progress bar, cancel button
19	 * - uploading: determinate progress bar 0-100%, cancel button
20	 * - complete: green flash for 1s, then removed from store
21	 * - error: red progress bar, retry + dismiss buttons
22	 */
23	export function UploadListItem({ fileId, onRetry }: UploadListItemProps) {
24	  const file = useUploadStore((s) => s.files.get(fileId));
25	  const cancelFile = useUploadStore((s) => s.cancelFile);
26	  const removeFile = useUploadStore((s) => s.removeFile);
27	  const retryFile = useUploadStore((s) => s.retryFile);
28	
29	  // Timer ref for completion flash (D-05, D-06)
30	  const completionTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();
31	
32	  // When status becomes 'complete', start 1000ms timer then remove (D-05)
33	  useEffect(() => {
34	    if (file?.status === 'complete') {
35	      completionTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
36	        removeFile(fileId);
37	      }, 1000);
38	    }
39	
40	    return () => {
41	      if (completionTimerRef.current) {
42	        clearTimeout(completionTimerRef.current);
43	      }
44	    };
45	  }, [file?.status, fileId, removeFile]);
46	
47	  // All hooks must be declared before any early return (React Rules of Hooks)
48	  const handleCancel = useCallback(() => {
49	    cancelFile(fileId);
50	    removeFile(fileId);
51	  }, [fileId, cancelFile, removeFile]);
52	
53	  const handleDismiss = useCallback(() => {
54	    removeFile(fileId);
55	  }, [fileId, removeFile]);
56	
57	  const handleRetry = useCallback(() => {
58	    retryFile(fileId);
59	    if (file?.file && onRetry) {
60	      onRetry(file.file);
61	    }
62	  }, [fileId, retryFile, file?.file, onRetry]);
63	
64	  if (!file) return null;
65	
66	  const isError = file.status === 'error';
67	  const isComplete = file.status === 'complete';
68	  const isEncrypting = file.status === 'encrypting';
69	
70	  const rowClassName = [
71	    'file-list-item',
72	    'upload-inline-row',
73	    isError ? 'upload-inline-row--error' : '',
74	    isComplete ? 'upload-inline-row--complete' : '',
75	  ]
76	    .filter(Boolean)
77	    .join(' ');
78	
79	  return (
80	    <div className={rowClassName} role="row">
81	      {/* Row top: icon + name + progress bar */}
82	      <div className="file-list-item-row-top" role="gridcell">
83	        <span className="file-list-item-icon upload-inline-icon" aria-hidden="true">
84	          {isError ? '[!]' : '[^]'}
85	        </span>
86	        <div className="upload-inline-name-wrapper">
87	          <span className="file-list-item-name">{file.filename}</span>
88	          <div
89	            className={`upload-inline-progress-track ${isEncrypting ? 'upload-inline-progress-track--pulse' : ''}`}
90	          >
91	            <div
92	              className="upload-inline-progress-fill"
93	              style={{ width: `${file.progress}%` }}
94	              data-status={file.status}
95	              role="progressbar"
96	              aria-valuenow={file.progress}
97	              aria-valuemin={0}
98	              aria-valuemax={100}
99	              aria-label={`Upload progress for ${file.filename}`}
100	            />
101	          </div>
102	        </div>
103	      </div>
104	
105	      {/* Row bottom: size + date/actions */}
106	      <div className="file-list-item-row-bottom">
107	        <span className="file-list-item-size" role="gridcell">
108	          {'--'}
109	        </span>
110	        <span className="file-list-item-date upload-inline-actions" role="gridcell">
111	          {/* Cancel button -- visible during encrypting/uploading */}
112	          {!isComplete && !isError && (
113	            <button
114	              type="button"
115	              className="upload-inline-btn"
116	              onClick={handleCancel}
117	              aria-label={`Cancel upload of ${file.filename}`}
118	              title="Cancel upload"
119	            >
120	              {'[x]'}
121	            </button>
122	          )}
123	          {/* Error state -- retry + dismiss */}
124	          {isError && (
125	            <>
126	              <button
127	                type="button"
128	                className="upload-inline-btn upload-inline-btn--retry"
129	                onClick={handleRetry}
130	                aria-label={`Retry upload of ${file.filename}`}
131	                title="Retry upload"
132	              >
133	                {'[R]'}
134	              </button>
135	              <button
136	                type="button"
137	                className="upload-inline-btn"
138	                onClick={handleDismiss}
139	                aria-label={`Dismiss failed upload of ${file.filename}`}
140	                title="Dismiss error"
141	              >
142	                {'[x]'}
143	              </button>
144	            </>
145	          )}
146	        </span>
147	      </div>
148	    </div>
149	  );
150	}
151

1	import { useCallback } from 'react';
2	import { logger } from '../lib/logger';
3	import { useUploadStore } from '../stores/upload.store';
4	import { useQuotaStore } from '../stores/quota.store';
5	import { useFolderStore } from '../stores/folder.store';
6	import { useVaultStore } from '../stores/vault.store';
7	import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';
8	import { getSdkClient, hasSdkClient, ensureFolderRegistered } from '../lib/sdk-provider';
9	import { getRootFolderState } from './folder-helpers';
10	import type { PendingReplacement } from '../stores/upload.store';
11	
12	export const MAX_FILE_SIZE = 100 * 1024 * 1024; // 100MB per FILE-01
13	
14	/**
15	 * Check whether a drag event carries external files from the OS (Finder/Explorer)
16	 * rather than an internal app drag (which sets application/json).
17	 */
18	export function isExternalFileDrag(dataTransfer: DataTransfer): boolean {
19	  return dataTransfer.types.includes('Files') && !dataTransfer.types.includes('application/json');
20	}
21	
22	/**
23	 * Hook for handling external file drops (from Finder/Explorer) anywhere in the app.
24	 *
25	 * Uses the SDK's uploadFile() for atomic encrypt -> upload -> register flow.
26	 * All IPNS state is managed by the SDK -- no dual-path conflicts.
27	 */
28	export function useDropUpload() {
29	  const uploadFiles = useUploadStore((s) => s.files);
30	  const isUploading =
31	    uploadFiles.size > 0 &&
32	    Array.from(uploadFiles.values()).some(
33	      (f) => f.status === 'encrypting' || f.status === 'uploading'
34	    );
35	
36	  const handleFileDrop = useCallback(async (files: File[], folderId: string): Promise<boolean> => {
37	    // Filter out oversized files
38	    const oversized = files.filter((f) => f.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE);
39	    if (oversized.length > 0) {
40	      logger.error(`[Upload] Files exceed 100MB limit: ${oversized.map((f) => f.name).join(', ')}`);
41	      return false;
42	    }
43	
44	    if (files.length === 0) return false;
45	
46	    // Check quota
47	    const totalSize = files.reduce((sum, f) => sum + f.size, 0);
48	    const quotaStore = useQuotaStore.getState();
49	    if (!quotaStore.canUpload(totalSize)) {
50	      logger.error('[Upload] Not enough storage space for these files');
51	      return false;
52	    }
53	
54	    // Check for duplicates within batch
55	    const batchNames = new Set<string>();
56	    for (const f of files) {
57	      if (batchNames.has(f.name)) {
58	        logger.error(`[Upload] Duplicate file name in selection: ${f.name}`);
59	        return false;
60	      }
61	      batchNames.add(f.name);
62	    }
63	
64	    // Identify which files already exist in the target folder
65	    const folder = useFolderStore.getState().folders[folderId];
66	    const existingByName = new Map<string, string>(); // name -> fileId
67	    const existingFolderNames = new Set<string>();
68	    if (folder) {
69	      for (const child of folder.children) {
70	        if (child.type === 'file') {
71	          existingByName.set(child.name, child.id);
72	        } else if (child.type === 'folder') {
73	          existingFolderNames.add(child.name);
74	        }
75	      }
76	    }
77	
78	    // Fail fast on file-folder name collisions
79	    const folderNameConflicts = files.filter((f) => existingFolderNames.has(f.name));
80	    if (folderNameConflicts.length > 0) {
81	      logger.error(
82	        `[Upload] Cannot upload file(s) with the same name as an existing folder: ${folderNameConflicts.map((f) => f.name).join(', ')}`
83	      );
84	      return false;
85	    }
86	
87	    const newFiles = files.filter((f) => !existingByName.has(f.name));
88	    const duplicateFiles = files.filter((f) => existingByName.has(f.name));
89	
90	    if (!hasSdkClient()) {
91	      logger.error('[Upload] SDK not initialized -- please log in again');
92	      return false;
93	    }
94	
95	    const client = getSdkClient();
96	
97	    // Resolve parent folder and ensure it's registered in the SDK
98	    const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
99	    const vault = useVaultStore.getState();
100	    const parentFolder =
101	      folderId === 'root' ? getRootFolderState(vault, folders) : folders[folderId];
102	    if (!parentFolder) {
103	      logger.error('[Upload] Parent folder not found');
104	      return false;
105	    }
106	    ensureFolderRegistered(parentFolder);
107	
108	    const uploadedCids: string[] = []; // Track for cleanup on failure
109	    let currentUploadId: string | undefined;
110	
111	    try {
112	      // Upload new files via SDK (atomic: encrypt -> IPFS -> FilePointer -> IPNS publish)
113	      for (const file of newFiles) {
114	        const uploadId = `upload-${file.name}-${Date.now()}`;
115	        currentUploadId = uploadId;
116	        useUploadStore.getState().addFile(uploadId, file.name, folderId, file);
117	
118	        if (useUploadStore.getState().files.get(uploadId)?.status === 'cancelled') {
119	          throw new Error('Upload cancelled by user');
120	        }
121	
122	        // Read File to Uint8Array
123	        const data = new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer());
124	
125	        const result = await client.uploadFile(
126	          parentFolder.ipnsName,
127	          data,
128	          file.name,
129	          file.type || 'application/octet-stream',
130	          (percent) => useUploadStore.getState().updateFileProgress(uploadId, percent)
131	        );
132	
133	        uploadedCids.push(result.cid);
134	        useUploadStore.getState().setFileComplete(uploadId);
135	      }
136	
137	      // Handle duplicate files: encrypt + upload to IPFS, then surface as pending replacements
138	      // (These don't get registered in the folder -- the user decides via the Replace dialog)
139	      // Duplicate files always upload to CipherBox relay (not BYO node) because
140	      // they're staged for the replacement dialog. Once the user confirms, the
141	      // replacement flow uses SDK's uploadFile() which respects pinning mode.
142	      if (duplicateFiles.length > 0) {
143	        const replacements: PendingReplacement[] = [];
144	
145	        // Hoist dynamic imports before the loop to avoid per-iteration async overhead
146	        const { encryptFile } = await import('../services/file-crypto.service');
147	        const { addToIpfs } = await import('../lib/api/ipfs');
148	
149	        for (const file of duplicateFiles) {
150	          const uploadId = `upload-${file.name}-${Date.now()}`;
151	          currentUploadId = uploadId;
152	          useUploadStore.getState().addFile(uploadId, file.name, folderId, file);
153	
154	          if (useUploadStore.getState().files.get(uploadId)?.status === 'cancelled') {
155	            throw new Error('Upload cancelled by user');
156	          }
157	
158	          // For duplicates, we only encrypt + upload to IPFS (don't register in folder)
159	          // Use the old upload service for this since SDK's uploadFile registers in folder
160	          const userPublicKey = useAuthStore.getState().vaultKeypair?.publicKey;
161	          if (!userPublicKey) throw new Error('No keypair available');
162	          const encrypted = await encryptFile(file, userPublicKey);
163	          // Pass a clean Uint8Array copy -- never use .buffer (may include extra bytes)
164	          const blob = new Blob([new Uint8Array(encrypted.ciphertext)], {
165	            type: 'application/octet-stream',
166	          });
167	
168	          const cancelToken = useUploadStore.getState().files.get(uploadId)?.cancelSource?.token;
169	          const ipfsResult = await addToIpfs(
170	            blob,
171	            (percent) => useUploadStore.getState().updateFileProgress(uploadId, percent),
172	            cancelToken
173	          );
174	
175	          uploadedCids.push(ipfsResult.cid);
176	          useUploadStore.getState().setFileComplete(uploadId);
177	
178	          const existingFileId = existingByName.get(file.name);
179	          if (existingFileId) {
180	            replacements.push({
181	              fileName: file.name,
182	              fileId: existingFileId,
183	              parentId: folderId,
184	              encryptedData: {
185	                cid: ipfsResult.cid,
186	                wrappedKey: encrypted.wrappedKey,
187	                iv: encrypted.iv,
188	                size: file.size,
189	                encryptionMode: encrypted.encryptionMode,
190	              },
191	            });
192	          }
193	        }
194	
195	        if (replacements.length > 0) {
196	          useUploadStore.getState().setPendingReplacements(replacements);
197	        }
198	      }
199	
200	      return true;
201	    } catch (err) {
202	      const message = (err as Error).message;
203	      if (message !== 'Upload cancelled by user' && currentUploadId) {
204	        useUploadStore.getState().setFileStatus(currentUploadId, 'error', message);
205	      }
206	      // Best-effort cleanup: unpin orphaned CIDs from failed upload
207	      if (uploadedCids.length > 0) {
208	        import('../lib/api/ipfs')
209	          .then(({ unpinFromIpfs }) => {
210	            for (const cid of uploadedCids) {
211	              unpinFromIpfs(cid).catch((err) =>
212	                logger.warn('[Upload] Unpin orphaned CID failed:', err)
213	              );
214	            }
215	          })
216	          .catch((err) => logger.warn('[Upload] Failed to load IPFS module for cleanup:', err));
217	      }
218	      return false;
219	    } finally {
220	      // Refresh quota from server
221	      await useQuotaStore.getState().fetchQuota();
222	    }
223	  }, []);
224	
225	  return { handleFileDrop, isUploading };
226	}
227

1	import { useCallback } from 'react';
2	import { uploadFiles, UploadedFile } from '../services/upload.service';
3	import { useUploadStore } from '../stores/upload.store';
4	import { useQuotaStore } from '../stores/quota.store';
5	import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';
6	
7	/**
8	 * React hook for file upload with encryption, progress tracking, and quota management.
9	 *
10	 * Derives batch-level fields from the per-file upload Map for backward compatibility.
11	 * The SDK client is used for post-upload operations (adding files to folders) via useFileOperations.
12	 */
13	export function useFileUpload() {
14	  const files = useUploadStore((s) => s.files);
15	  const reset = useUploadStore((s) => s.reset);
16	
17	  const { usedBytes, limitBytes, remainingBytes, canUpload, fetchQuota } = useQuotaStore();
18	  const { vaultKeypair } = useAuthStore();
19	
20	  // Derive batch-level status from per-file Map
21	  const fileEntries = Array.from(files.values());
22	  const hasActiveUpload = fileEntries.some(
23	    (f) => f.status === 'encrypting' || f.status === 'uploading'
24	  );
25	  const hasError = fileEntries.some((f) => f.status === 'error');
26	  const activeFile = fileEntries.find((f) => f.status === 'encrypting' || f.status === 'uploading');
27	
28	  const upload = useCallback(
29	    async (filesToUpload: File[]): Promise<UploadedFile[]> => {
30	      if (!vaultKeypair) {
31	        throw new Error('No keypair available - please log in again');
32	      }
33	
34	      // Refresh quota before upload
35	      await fetchQuota();
36	
37	      return uploadFiles(filesToUpload, vaultKeypair.publicKey);
38	    },
39	    [vaultKeypair, fetchQuota]
40	  );
41	
42	  return {
43	    // State (derived from per-file Map)
44	    status: hasError
45	      ? ('error' as const)
46	      : hasActiveUpload
47	        ? ('uploading' as const)
48	        : ('idle' as const),
49	    progress: activeFile?.progress ?? 0,
50	    currentFile: activeFile?.filename ?? null,
51	    totalFiles: fileEntries.length,
52	    completedFiles: fileEntries.filter((f) => f.status === 'complete').length,
53	    error: fileEntries.find((f) => f.error)?.error ?? null,
54	    isUploading: hasActiveUpload,
55	
56	    // Quota
57	    usedBytes,
58	    limitBytes,
59	    remainingBytes,
60	    canUpload,
61	
62	    // Actions
63	    upload,
64	    cancel: () => {
65	      // Cancel all active uploads
66	      for (const f of files.values()) {
67	        if (f.status === 'encrypting' || f.status === 'uploading') {
68	          useUploadStore.getState().cancelFile(f.id);
69	        }
70	      }
71	    },
72	    reset,
73	  };
74	}
75

1	import { encryptFile, EncryptedFileResult } from './file-crypto.service';
2	import { addToIpfs, AddResponse } from '../lib/api/ipfs';
3	import { useQuotaStore } from '../stores/quota.store';
4	import { useUploadStore } from '../stores/upload.store';
5	import { CancelToken } from 'axios';
6	import { logger } from '../lib/logger';
7	
8	const MAX_RETRIES = 3;
9	const RETRY_BASE_DELAY = 500;
10	
11	export type UploadedFile = {
12	  cid: string;
13	  size: number;
14	  iv: string;
15	  wrappedKey: string;
16	  originalName: string;
17	  originalSize: number;
18	  encryptionMode: 'GCM' | 'CTR';
19	};
20	
21	/**
22	 * Retry wrapper with exponential backoff.
23	 * Does not retry cancelled operations.
24	 */
25	async function withRetry<T>(
26	  fn: () => Promise<T>,
27	  maxRetries: number = MAX_RETRIES,
28	  baseDelay: number = RETRY_BASE_DELAY
29	): Promise<T> {
30	  let lastError: Error = new Error('Retry failed');
31	
32	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < maxRetries; attempt++) {
33	    try {
34	      return await fn();
35	    } catch (error) {
36	      lastError = error as Error;
37	      // Don't retry if cancelled
38	      if ((error as Error).message === 'Upload cancelled by user') {
39	        throw error;
40	      }
41	      if (attempt < maxRetries - 1) {
42	        const delay = baseDelay * Math.pow(2, attempt);
43	        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, delay));
44	      }
45	    }
46	  }
47	
48	  throw lastError;
49	}
50	
51	/**
52	 * Upload a single file: encrypt then upload to IPFS.
53	 */
54	export async function uploadFile(
55	  file: File,
56	  userPublicKey: Uint8Array,
57	  onProgress?: (percent: number) => void,
58	  cancelToken?: CancelToken
59	): Promise<UploadedFile> {
60	  // 1. Encrypt the file
61	  const encrypted: EncryptedFileResult = await encryptFile(file, userPublicKey);
62	
63	  // 2. Upload to IPFS with retry
64	  // Cast to ArrayBuffer for TypeScript 5.9 compatibility (Uint8Array.buffer is ArrayBufferLike)
65	  const blob = new Blob([encrypted.ciphertext.buffer as ArrayBuffer], {
66	    type: 'application/octet-stream',
67	  });
68	  const result: AddResponse = await withRetry(() => addToIpfs(blob, onProgress, cancelToken));
69	
70	  return {
71	    cid: result.cid,
72	    size: result.size,
73	    iv: encrypted.iv,
74	    wrappedKey: encrypted.wrappedKey,
75	    originalName: file.name,
76	    originalSize: encrypted.originalSize,
77	    encryptionMode: encrypted.encryptionMode,
78	  };
79	}
80	
81	/**
82	 * Upload multiple files sequentially.
83	 * Pre-checks quota and tracks progress via upload store (per-file tracking).
84	 */
85	export async function uploadFiles(
86	  files: File[],
87	  userPublicKey: Uint8Array
88	): Promise<UploadedFile[]> {
89	  const quotaStore = useQuotaStore.getState();
90	
91	  // Calculate total size
92	  const totalSize = files.reduce((sum, f) => sum + f.size, 0);
93	
94	  // Pre-check quota
95	  if (!quotaStore.canUpload(totalSize)) {
96	    throw new Error(
97	      `Not enough space (${Math.round(quotaStore.usedBytes / 1024 / 1024)} of ${Math.round(quotaStore.limitBytes / 1024 / 1024)}MB used)`
98	    );
99	  }
100	
101	  const results: UploadedFile[] = [];
102	
103	  try {
104	    // Sequential uploads per CONTEXT.md decision
105	    for (const file of files) {
106	      const uploadId = `upload-${file.name}-${Date.now()}`;
107	      useUploadStore.getState().addFile(uploadId, file.name, '', file);
108	
109	      const cancelSource = useUploadStore.getState().files.get(uploadId)?.cancelSource;
110	      if (useUploadStore.getState().files.get(uploadId)?.status === 'cancelled') {
111	        throw new Error('Upload cancelled by user');
112	      }
113	
114	      const result = await uploadFile(
115	        file,
116	        userPublicKey,
117	        (percent) => useUploadStore.getState().updateFileProgress(uploadId, percent),
118	        cancelSource?.token
119	      );
120	
121	      results.push(result);
122	      useUploadStore.getState().setFileComplete(uploadId);
123	    }
124	
125	    return results;
126	  } catch (error) {
127	    const message = (error as Error).message;
128	    if (message !== 'Upload cancelled by user') {
129	      logger.error('[Upload] Upload failed:', error);
130	    }
131	    throw error;
132	  } finally {
133	    // Refresh quota from server (authoritative source after atomic upload)
134	    await useQuotaStore.getState().fetchQuota();
135	  }
136	}
137

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/UploadListItem.tsx",
  "old_string": "  const cancelFile = useUploadStore((s) => s.cancelFile);\n  const removeFile = useUploadStore((s) => s.removeFile);\n  const retryFile = useUploadStore((s) => s.retryFile);\n\n  // Timer ref for completion flash (D-05, D-06)\n  const completionTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();\n\n  // When status becomes 'complete', start 1000ms timer then remove (D-05)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (file?.status === 'complete') {\n      completionTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {\n        removeFile(fileId);\n      }, 1000);\n    }\n\n    return () => {\n      if (completionTimerRef.current) {\n        clearTimeout(completionTimerRef.current);\n      }\n    };\n  }, [file?.status, fileId, removeFile]);\n\n  // All hooks must be declared before any early return (React Rules of Hooks)\n  const handleCancel = useCallback(() => {\n    cancelFile(fileId);\n    removeFile(fileId);\n  }, [fileId, cancelFile, removeFile]);\n\n  const handleDismiss = useCallback(() => {\n    removeFile(fileId);\n  }, [fileId, removeFile]);\n\n  const handleRetry = useCallback(() => {\n    retryFile(fileId);\n    if (file?.file && onRetry) {\n      onRetry(file.file);\n    }\n  }, [fileId, retryFile, file?.file, onRetry]);",
  "new_string": "  const cancelFile = useUploadStore((s) => s.cancelFile);\n  const removeFile = useUploadStore((s) => s.removeFile);\n  const retryFile = useUploadStore((s) => s.retryFile);\n\n  const completionTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();\n\n  // Auto-remove after 1s green flash on completion (D-05)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (file?.status === 'complete') {\n      completionTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {\n        removeFile(fileId);\n      }, 1000);\n    }\n\n    return () => {\n      if (completionTimerRef.current) {\n        clearTimeout(completionTimerRef.current);\n      }\n    };\n  }, [file?.status, fileId, removeFile]);\n\n  const handleCancel = useCallback(() => {\n    cancelFile(fileId);\n  }, [fileId, cancelFile]);\n\n  const handleDismiss = useCallback(() => {\n    removeFile(fileId);\n  }, [fileId, removeFile]);\n\n  const handleRetry = useCallback(() => {\n    retryFile(fileId);\n    if (file?.file && onRetry) {\n      onRetry(file.file);\n    }\n  }, [fileId, retryFile, file?.file, onRetry]);"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/FileList.tsx",
  "old_string": "  // Subscribe to upload files for this folder only (IDs + filenames + status, not progress).\n  // Progress updates re-render individual UploadListItem rows via their own fine-grained selectors.\n  const uploadEntries = useUploadStore((s) => {\n    const entries: { id: string; filename: string; status: PerFileUpload['status'] }[] = [];\n    for (const f of s.files.values()) {\n      if (f.targetFolderId === parentId && f.status !== 'cancelled') {\n        entries.push({ id: f.id, filename: f.filename, status: f.status });\n      }\n    }\n    return entries;\n  });",
  "new_string": "  // Subscribe to upload entries for this folder only (IDs + filenames + status, not progress).\n  // Progress updates re-render individual UploadListItem rows via their own fine-grained selectors.\n  const uploadEntries = useUploadStore(\n    (s) => {\n      const entries: { id: string; filename: string; status: PerFileUpload['status'] }[] = [];\n      for (const f of s.files.values()) {\n        if (f.targetFolderId === parentId && f.status !== 'cancelled') {\n          entries.push({ id: f.id, filename: f.filename, status: f.status });\n        }\n      }\n      return entries;\n    },\n    (a, b) => {\n      if (a.length !== b.length) return false;\n      for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {\n        if (a[i].id !== b[i].id || a[i].status !== b[i].status) return false;\n      }\n      return true;\n    }\n  );"
}

  "old_string": "\n\n/**\n * Upload multiple files sequentially.\n * Pre-checks quota and tracks progress via upload store (per-file tracking).\n */\nexport async function uploadFiles(\n  files: File[],\n  userPublicKey: Uint8Array\n): Promise<UploadedFile[]> {\n  const quotaStore = useQuotaStore.getState();\n\n  // Calculate total size\n  const totalSize = files.reduce((sum, f) => sum + f.size, 0);\n\n  // Pre-check quota\n  if (!quotaStore.canUpload(totalSize)) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `Not enough space (${Math.round(quotaStore.usedBytes / 1024 / 1024)} of ${Math.round(quotaStore.limitBytes / 1024 / 1024)}MB used)`\n    );\n  }\n\n  const results: UploadedFile[] = [];\n\n  try {\n    // Sequential uploads per CONTEXT.md decision\n    for (const file of files) {\n      const uploadId = createUploadId(file.name);\n      useUploadStore.getState().addFile(uploadId, file.name, '', file);\n\n      const cancelSource = useUploadStore.getState().files.get(uploadId)?.cancelSource;\n      if (useUploadStore.getState().files.get(uploadId)?.status === 'cancelled') {\n        throw new Error('Upload cancelled by user');\n      }\n\n      const result = await uploadFile(\n        file,\n        userPublicKey,\n        (percent) => useUploadStore.getState().updateFileProgress(uploadId, percent),\n        cancelSource?.token\n      );\n\n      results.push(result);\n      useUploadStore.getState().setFileStatus(uploadId, 'complete');\n    }\n\n    return results;\n  } catch (error) {\n    const message = (error as Error).message;\n    if (message !== 'Upload cancelled by user') {\n      logger.error('[Upload] Upload failed:', error);\n    }\n    throw error;\n  } finally {\n    // Refresh quota from server (authoritative source after atomic upload)\n    await useQuotaStore.getState().fetchQuota();\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": ""
}

1	import { encryptFile, EncryptedFileResult } from './file-crypto.service';
2	import { addToIpfs, AddResponse } from '../lib/api/ipfs';
3	import { useQuotaStore } from '../stores/quota.store';
4	import { CancelToken } from 'axios';
5	
6	const MAX_RETRIES = 3;
7	const RETRY_BASE_DELAY = 500;
8	
9	export type UploadedFile = {
10	  cid: string;
11	  size: number;
12	  iv: string;
13	  wrappedKey: string;
14	  originalName: string;
15	  originalSize: number;
16	  encryptionMode: 'GCM' | 'CTR';
17	};
18	
19	/**
20	 * Retry wrapper with exponential backoff.
21	 * Does not retry cancelled operations.
22	 */
23	async function withRetry<T>(
24	  fn: () => Promise<T>,
25	  maxRetries: number = MAX_RETRIES,
26	  baseDelay: number = RETRY_BASE_DELAY
27	): Promise<T> {
28	  let lastError: Error = new Error('Retry failed');
29	
30	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < maxRetries; attempt++) {
31	    try {
32	      return await fn();
33	    } catch (error) {
34	      lastError = error as Error;
35	      // Don't retry if cancelled
36	      if ((error as Error).message === 'Upload cancelled by user') {
37	        throw error;
38	      }
39	      if (attempt < maxRetries - 1) {
40	        const delay = baseDelay * Math.pow(2, attempt);
41	        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, delay));
42	      }
43	    }
44	  }
45	
46	  throw lastError;
47	}
48	
49	/**
50	 * Upload a single file: encrypt then upload to IPFS.
51	 */
52	export async function uploadFile(
53	  file: File,
54	  userPublicKey: Uint8Array,
55	  onProgress?: (percent: number) => void,
56	  cancelToken?: CancelToken
57	): Promise<UploadedFile> {
58	  // 1. Encrypt the file
59	  const encrypted: EncryptedFileResult = await encryptFile(file, userPublicKey);
60	
61	  // 2. Upload to IPFS with retry
62	  // Cast to ArrayBuffer for TypeScript 5.9 compatibility (Uint8Array.buffer is ArrayBufferLike)
63	  const blob = new Blob([encrypted.ciphertext.buffer as ArrayBuffer], {
64	    type: 'application/octet-stream',
65	  });
66	  const result: AddResponse = await withRetry(() => addToIpfs(blob, onProgress, cancelToken));
67	
68	  return {
69	    cid: result.cid,
70	    size: result.size,
71	    iv: encrypted.iv,
72	    wrappedKey: encrypted.wrappedKey,
73	    originalName: file.name,
74	    originalSize: encrypted.originalSize,
75	    encryptionMode: encrypted.encryptionMode,
76	  };
77	}
```

> AGENT

The test at line 132 doesn't call `cancelFile` directly — it tests the error recovery path which checks `status === 'cancelled'`. That path is in `useDropUpload`'s catch block. The cancel happens via the `cancelSource` token (Axios CancelToken), not via `cancelFile`. The `simulateErrorRecovery` function only calls `setFileStatus` — not `cancelFile`. So the test still passes because the file was never cancelled via `cancelFile`.

> AGENT

The `status === 'cancelled'` checks in `useDropUpload.ts:118,153` are race-condition guards — they check if the user cancelled between `addFile` and the actual upload starting. Since `cancelFile` now deletes the entry, `files.get(uploadId)` returns `undefined`, and `undefined?.status === 'cancelled'` is `false`. This means the guard no longer catches immediate cancellations.

The fix: check if the entry is missing (deleted = cancelled):

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01Mfw8bA9JFZ4LaN7CfMwJ35
```json
{
  "replace_all": true,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useDropUpload.ts",
  "old_string": "        if (useUploadStore.getState().files.get(uploadId)?.status === 'cancelled') {\n          throw new Error('Upload cancelled by user');\n        }",
  "new_string": "        if (!useUploadStore.getState().files.get(uploadId)) {\n          throw new Error('Upload cancelled by user');\n        }"
}
```

> TOOL

> AGENT

Zustand v5. In v5, `useStore` accepts a second equality argument directly. The issue is that `useUploadStore` was created with `create<UploadState>` which returns a bound hook. The two-argument form needs `useStore` from `zustand`. But actually in Zustand v5, the hook returned by `create` should accept a second argument. The TypeScript issue is likely that the overload inference fails. Let me use `useShallow` from `zustand/react/shallow` instead, which is the idiomatic v5 approach. Actually, the simplest fix is to extract the type explicitly:

> TOOL

type ReadonlyStoreApi<T> = Pick<StoreApi<T>, 'getState' | 'getInitialState' | 'subscribe'>;
export declare function useStoreWithEqualityFn<S extends ReadonlyStoreApi<unknown>>(api: S): ExtractState<S>;
export declare function useStoreWithEqualityFn<S extends ReadonlyStoreApi<unknown>, U>(api: S, selector: (state: ExtractState<S>) => U, equalityFn?: (a: U, b: U) => boolean): U;
export type UseBoundStoreWithEqualityFn<S extends ReadonlyStoreApi<unknown>> = {
    (): ExtractState<S>;
    <U>(selector: (state: ExtractState<S>) => U, equalityFn?: (a: U, b: U) => boolean): U;
} & S;
type CreateWithEqualityFn = {
    <T, Mos extends [StoreMutatorIdentifier, unknown][] = []>(initializer: StateCreator<T, [], Mos>, defaultEqualityFn?: <U>(a: U, b: U) => boolean): UseBoundStoreWithEqualityFn<Mutate<StoreApi<T>, Mos>>;
    <T>(): <Mos extends [StoreMutatorIdentifier, unknown][] = []>(initializer: StateCreator<T, [], Mos>, defaultEqualityFn?: <U>(a: U, b: U) => boolean) => UseBoundStoreWithEqualityFn<Mutate<StoreApi<T>, Mos>>;
};
export declare const createWithEqualityFn: CreateWithEqualityFn;
export {};

  "old_string": "  // Subscribe to upload entries for this folder only (IDs + filenames + status, not progress).\n  // Progress updates re-render individual UploadListItem rows via their own fine-grained selectors.\n  const uploadEntries = useUploadStore(\n    (s) => {\n      const entries: { id: string; filename: string; status: PerFileUpload['status'] }[] = [];\n      for (const f of s.files.values()) {\n        if (f.targetFolderId === parentId) {\n          entries.push({ id: f.id, filename: f.filename, status: f.status });\n        }\n      }\n      return entries;\n    },\n    (a, b) => {\n      if (a.length !== b.length) return false;\n      for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {\n        if (a[i].id !== b[i].id || a[i].status !== b[i].status) return false;\n      }\n      return true;\n    }\n  );",
  "new_string": "  // Subscribe to upload entries for this folder only (IDs + status, not progress).\n  // Progress updates re-render individual UploadListItem rows via their own fine-grained selectors.\n  // Custom equality via ref prevents re-renders on every progress tick.\n  type UploadEntry = { id: string; filename: string; status: PerFileUpload['status'] };\n  const prevEntriesRef = useRef<UploadEntry[]>([]);\n  const uploadEntries = useUploadStore((s) => {\n    const entries: UploadEntry[] = [];\n    for (const f of s.files.values()) {\n      if (f.targetFolderId === parentId) {\n        entries.push({ id: f.id, filename: f.filename, status: f.status });\n      }\n    }\n    const prev = prevEntriesRef.current;\n    if (\n      prev.length === entries.length &&\n      prev.every((p, i) => p.id === entries[i].id && p.status === entries[i].status)\n    ) {\n      return prev;\n    }\n    prevEntriesRef.current = entries;\n    return entries;\n  });"
}
